</div><div class="shopify-section shopify-section--blog-feed shopify-section--no-margin hide" id="shopify-section-1521600208938"><script>
    (function() {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        getFeed();
      });

      function getFeed() {
        var feedUrl = 'https://blog.sweelee.com/feed/';
        if (!feedUrl) {
          return;
        }

        var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
        xhr.responseType = 'document';
        xhr.onreadystatechange = function() {
          if (this.readyState === 4 && this.status === 200) {
            var htmlFeed = parseFeed(xhr.responseXML);
            createCarousel(htmlFeed);
          }
        };
        xhr.open('GET', feedUrl, true);
        xhr.send();
      }

      function createCarousel(html) {
        if (!html.length) {
          return document.getElementById('blogFeed').remove();
        }

        document.querySelector('#blogFeed .carousel').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
        if (window.lazyLoadInstance) {
          window.lazyLoadInstance.update();
        }

        setTimeout(function() {
          Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.section-container--blog-feed, .shopify-section--blog-feed'), function(blogContainer) {
            blogContainer.classList.remove('hide');
          });
          window.carousel.initArticle('#blogFeed .carousel');
        }, 300);
      }

      function parseFeed(xmlFeed) {
        var items = xmlFeed.getElementsByTagName('item');
        if (!items) {
          return;
        }
        var html = '';
        for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
          if (!items[i]) {
            return;
          }
          var item = items[i],
            gaTracking = `
            
              data-ga-on="click" data-ga-event-category="Landing Pages" data-ga-event-action="Clicked Blog Feed Card" data-ga-event-label="${item.getElementsByTagName('link')[0].textContent}"
            `;
          html += `
            <div class="carousel-cell">
              <div class="article-card">
                <div class="article-card__image">
                  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="${item.getElementsByTagName('link')[0].textContent}" aria-label="${item.getElementsByTagName('title')[0].textContent}" ${gaTracking}>
                    <img data-lazyload data-src="${item.getElementsByTagName('thumbnail')[0].textContent}" alt="${item.getElementsByTagName('title')[0].textContent}">
                  </a>
                </div>
                <div class="article-card__copy">
                  <h5 class="article-card__pre-heading">#${item.getElementsByTagName('category')[0].textContent}</h5>
                  <h4 class="article-card__heading">
                    <a target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="${item.getElementsByTagName('link')[0].textContent}" ${gaTracking}>${item.getElementsByTagName('title')[0].textContent}</a>
                  </h4>
                  <p class="article-card__excerpt">
                    <span class="article-card__excerpt-copy">${item.getElementsByTagName('excerpt')[0].textContent.replace('[&#8230;]', '…')}</span>
                    <a href="${item.getElementsByTagName('link')[0].textContent}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" ${gaTracking}>Read more</a>
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>`;
        }
        return html;
      }
    })();
  </script>

<script>
(function() {
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    var section = document.getElementById('instagram-feed-section'),
      instagramFeed = document.getElementById('instagram-feed'),
      isSmall = matchMedia('screen and (max-width: 47.9375em)').matches,
      template = `
        <a href="[url]" target="_blank" rel="noopener" title="[title]"
           data-ga-on="click" data-ga-event-category="Landing Pages" data-ga-event-action="Clicked Instagram Feed Link" data-ga-event-label="[url]"
           class="carousel-cell instagram-feed-link">
          <div class="instagram-feed-link__content">
            <img data-lazyload data-src="[image]" alt="[title]">
            <div class="instagram-feed-link__copy-container show-for-small-only">
              <p class="instagram-feed-link__copy text-single last">[title]</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </a>`;

    Shopify.utils.getJSON('https://api.instagram.com/v1/users/648407677/media/recent?access_token=648407677.d5d3249.a6bd53f2da3f4de294b6072b921af67a&count=20', function(feed) {
      var html = '',
        requiredTags = '',
        itemCount = 0;

      Array.prototype.forEach.call(feed.data, function(item) {
        var hasTags = Array.prototype.every.call(requiredTags.split(','), function(tag) {
          return Array.prototype.includes.call(item.tags, tag);
        });
        if (requiredTags === '' || hasTags) {
          itemCount++;
          if (isSmall && itemCount > 5) {
            return;
          }
          html += Shopify.utils.render(template, {
            title: item.caption.text,
            image: item.images.standard_resolution.url,
            url: item.link
          });
        }
      });
      if (!isSmall && itemCount < 5) {
        html += html;
      }
      section.classList.remove('hide');
      instagramFeed.innerHTML = html;
      instagramFeed.classList.add('carousel');
      if (window.lazyLoadInstance) {
        window.lazyLoadInstance.update();
      }
      window.carousel.initInstagram(instagramFeed);
    });
  });
})();
</script>
